   611→        @mcp.tool()
   612→        def surrealdb_query(query: str) -> str:
   613→            """Execute a custom SurrealQL query against the vault graph database.
   614→
   615→            Args:
   616→                query: SurrealQL query string (automatically prefixed with USE NS/DB)
   617→
   618→            Returns:
   619→                Query results as JSON
   620→
   621→            Example queries:
   622→            - "SELECT * FROM paper WHERE tags CONTAINS 'ai' LIMIT 10;"
   623→            - "SELECT count() FROM links GROUP BY out;"
   624→            - "SELECT * FROM paper->links->concept WHERE out.title = 'agentic-ai';"
   625→            """
   626→            try:
   627→                # Prepend USE statements
   628→                full_query = f"USE NS {surrealdb.namespace}; USE DB {surrealdb.database}; {query}"
   629→                results = surrealdb._execute_query(full_query)
   630→                return json.dumps(results, indent=2, default=str)
   631→            except Exception as e:
   632→                logger.error(f"Query failed: {e}")
   633→                return f"Error executing query: {e}"

`src/cohezion/swarm/workflows/debate_protocol.py` (1)\n- Test: `tests/lint/test_ruf012_classvar.py` (new)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n\n- Fix pattern: Add `from typing import ClassVar` import, then annotate `ATTR: ClassVar[list[str]] = [...]` or `ATTR: ClassVar[dict[str, float]] = {...}`\n- These are all true class constants (uppercase names, never mutated after class definition). `ClassVar` is the correct annotation.\n- Do NOT move these to `__init__` — they are intentional class-level constants shared across all instances\n- Verify each file's existing imports before adding `ClassVar`\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n\n- [ ] `uv run ruff check src/cohezion/ --select RUF012` reports 0 errors\n- [ ] All existing tests still pass (`uv run pytest tests/ -q -o \"addopts=\"`)\n- [ ] New regression test verifies RUF012 count is 0\n\n**Verify:**\n\n- `uv run ruff check src/cohezion/ --select RUF012 2>&1 | tail -1` — \"Found 0 errors\"\n- `uv run pytest tests/ -q -o \"addopts=\" -p no:cacheprovider --tb=line 2>&1 | tail -3` — 0 failures\n\n### Task 2: Fix RUF006 Asyncio Dangling Tasks\n\n**Objective:** Store references to all 15 fire-and-forget `asyncio.create_task()` calls to prevent tasks from being garbage-collected mid-execution. Each class needs a `_background_tasks: set[asyncio.Task]` attribute and the pattern `task = asyncio.create_task(...); self._background_tasks.add(task); task.add_done_callback(self._background_tasks.discard)`.\n\n**Dependencies:** None (can run in parallel with Task 1)\n\n**Files (10 files, all Modify):**\n\n- `src/cohezion/agents/base.py` (3 violations: lines 129, 560, 819)\n- `src/cohezion/cache/semantic_cache.py` (1: line 293)\n- `src/cohezion/compound/executor.py` (1: line 840 — uses `asyncio.ensure_future`)\n- `src/cohezion/compound/thermal_history_persistence.py` (1: line 93)\n- `src/cohezion/compound/thermal_trend_predictor.py` (1: line 113)\n- `src/cohezion/core/connection_pool.py` (2: lines 106, 277)\n- `src/cohezion/core/multimodal_bridge.py` (1: line 46)\n- `src/cohezion/core/task_manager.py` (1: line 166)\n- `src/cohezion/cost_optimization/budget_enforcer.py` (1: line 303)\n- `src/cohezion/cost_optimization/cost_tracker.py` (1: line 186 — uses `loop.create_task`)\n- `src/cohezion/reliability/quantum_performance_monitor.py` (2: lines 263, 467)\n- Test: `tests/lint/test_ruf006_dangling_tasks.py` (new)\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n\n- Standard pattern for each class:\n  ```python\n  def __init__(self):\n      self._background_tasks: set[asyncio.Task] = set()\n\n  # Then at each create_task site:\n  task = asyncio.create_task(self._some_coroutine())\n  self._background_tasks.add(task)\n  task.add_done_callback(self._background_tasks.discard)\n  ```\n- For `executor.py` line 840 which uses `asyncio.ensure_future`: convert to `asyncio.create_task` and apply same pattern\n- For `cost_tracker.py` line 186 which uses `loop.create_task`: same pattern but with `loop.create_task`\n- For `connection_pool.py` line 106 (in a loop): collect all tasks in a list, then add to set\n- Some classes already have `__init__` methods — add the set there. Some don't — add `__init__` or use class-level `_background_tasks: set = set()` (but this would trigger RUF012, so must be in `__init__`)\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n\n- [ ] `uv run ruff check src/cohezion/ --select RUF006` reports 0 errors\n- [ ] All existing tests still pass\n- [ ] New regression test verifies RUF006 count is 0\n\n**Verify:**\n\n- `uv run ruff check src/cohezion/ --select RUF006 2>&1 | tail -1` — \"Found 0 errors\"\n- `uv run pytest tests/ -q -o \"addopts=\" -p no:cacheprovider --tb=line 2>&1 | tail -3` — 0 failures\n\n### Task 3: Fix SurrealDB Integration Test Event Loop Issues\n\n**Objective:** Fix the 6 failures + 23 errors in `tests/integration/test_surreal_persistence.py` caused by `scope=\"module\"` async fixture creating a SurrealDB client on one event loop while individual tests run on different loops.\n\n**Dependencies:** None (can run in parallel with Tasks 1-2)\n\n**Files:**\n\n- Modify: `tests/integration/test_surreal_persistence.py`\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n\n- Root cause: `@pytest.fixture(scope=\"module\") async def surreal_client()` creates the client+connection in one event loop. With `pytest-asyncio` (strict mode), each test function gets its own event loop. The SurrealDB client's internal websocket Future is bound to the fixture's loop.\n- Fix: Change fixture from `scope=\"module\"` to `scope=\"function\"` (default). Each test gets its own client+connection on its own event loop.\n- To avoid reconnecting for every test (slow), add a module-level check that SurrealDB is reachable, then let each test create its own client.\n- Add `pytestmark = [pytest.mark.asyncio, pytest.mark.integration]` so tests can be selectively run with `-m integration`\n- Keep the `SKIP_INTEGRATION` env var check\n- Add a lightweight sync connectivity check at module level that skips the entire module if SurrealDB isn't reachable\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n\n- [ ] `uv run pytest tests/integration/test_surreal_persistence.py -v --tb=short` — all tests pass (0 failures, 0 errors)\n- [ ] Tests still skip cleanly when SurrealDB is not running\n- [ ] Full test suite still passes\n\n**Verify:**\n\n- `uv run pytest tests/integration/test_surreal_persistence.py -q --tb=short 2>&1 | tail -5` — 0 failures\n- `uv run pytest tests/ -q -o \"addopts=\" -p no:cacheprovider --tb=line 2>&1 | tail -3` — 0 failures\n\n### Task 4: Add Persistent Memory Tools to cloud-vault-mcp\n\n**Objective:** Add 4 new MCP tools (`memory_search`, `memory_save`, `memory_get`, `memory_timeline`) to the cloud-vault-mcp server to replace the lost PILOT `mem-search` functionality. These tools store observations as JSONL entries in `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/memory/observations.jsonl` and support search by text, retrieval by ID, and timeline navigation.\n\n**Dependencies:** None\n\n**Files:**\n\n- Create: `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/memory_store.py` — MemoryStore class with JSONL backend\n- Modify: `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/server.py` — register 4 new tools\n- Create: `cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_memory_store.py` — unit tests for MemoryStore\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n\n- **Storage format:** JSONL file at `{vault_path}/memory/observations.jsonl`. Each line is a JSON object:\n  ```json\n  {\"id\": 1, \"timestamp\": \"2026-02-23T10:00:00Z\", \"text\": \"...\", \"title\": \"...\", \"type\": \"discovery\", \"project\": \"cohezion\", \"tags\": []}\n  ```\n- **ID assignment:** Auto-incrementing integer (max ID from file + 1). Thread-safe via file lock.\n- **Search:** Full-text substring match on `text` + `title` fields, with optional filters: `type`, `project`, `dateStart`, `dateEnd`, `limit`. Returns index entries (id, title, timestamp, snippet) for token efficiency.\n- **Get:** Fetch full observation details by list of IDs.\n- **Timeline:** Given an anchor ID or query, return chronological observations around it (configurable `depth_before`, `depth_after`).\n- **Tool signatures** (matching old mem-search API from `~/.claude/rules/memory.md`):\n  - `memory_search(query: str, limit: int = 20, type: str = \"\", project: str = \"\", dateStart: str = \"\", dateEnd: str = \"\") → str`\n  - `memory_save(text: str, title: str = \"\", project: str = \"cohezion\", type: str = \"discovery\") → str`\n  - `memory_get(ids: list[int]) → str`\n  - `memory_timeline(anchor: int = 0, query: str = \"\", depth_before: int = 5, depth_after: int = 5) → str`\n- Follow existing server.py pattern: import module, create instance, register tools with `@mcp.tool()`\n- JSONL chosen over SurrealDB to avoid adding another DB dependency and to ensure the memory file is portable/inspectable\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n\n- [ ] 4 new MCP tools registered in cloud-vault-mcp server\n- [ ] MemoryStore handles save, search, get, timeline operations\n- [ ] JSONL file created at `{vault_path}/memory/observations.jsonl`\n- [ ] Unit tests cover all 4 operations + edge cases (empty store, missing IDs, date filtering)\n- [ ] Tools return JSON matching the 3-layer workflow from `memory.md`\n\n**Verify:**\n\n- `uv run pytest cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_memory_store.py -q` — all tests pass\n- `uv run ruff check cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/memory_store.py` — 0 lint errors\n\n### Task 5: Update Memory Rules and Verify Integration\n\n**Objective:** Update `~/.claude/rules/memory.md` to reference the new cloud-vault-mcp memory tools instead of the old PILOT mem-search tools. Update `~/.claude/rules/mcp-cli.md` to remove the stale `mem-search` reference. Verify end-to-end that the MCP server exposes the new tools.\n\n**Dependencies:** Task 4\n\n**Files:**\n\n- Modify: `~/.claude/rules/memory.md` — update tool names and examples\n- Modify: `~/.claude/rules/mcp-cli.md` — remove `mem-search` from the Pilot Core Servers list\n- Modify: `memory/MEMORY.md` — update Ongoing Issues to mark mem-search as resolved\n\n**Key Decisions / Notes:**\n\n- Tool name mapping: `search` → `memory_search`, `get_observations` → `memory_get`, `save_memory` → `memory_save`, `timeline` → `memory_timeline`\n- The 3-layer workflow (search → timeline → get) stays the same\n- Update examples to use the new tool names accessible via ToolSearch (`mcp__cohezion-vault__memory_search`, etc.)\n- Remove `mem-search` from the \"Pilot Core Servers\" row in `mcp-cli.md`\n\n**Definition of Done:**\n\n- [ ] `~/.claude/rules/memory.md` references new tool names\n- [ ] `~/.claude/rules/mcp-cli.md` no longer mentions `mem-search`\n- [ ] `memory/MEMORY.md` updated — mem-search marked resolved\n- [ ] cloud-vault-mcp server restart shows 4 new tools\n\n**Verify:**\n\n- `grep -c \"mem-search\" ~/.claude/rules/memory.md ~/.claude/rules/mcp-cli.md` — 0 matches\n- `grep \"memory_search\\|memory_save\\|memory_get\\|memory_timeline\" ~/.claude/rules/memory.md` — all 4 present\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n- **Unit tests:** Each task has targeted unit/lint regression tests\n- **Integration tests:** Task 3 directly fixes integration test suite; Task 4 tests MemoryStore with real JSONL I/O\n- **Manual verification:**\n  1. After Task 1+2: `uv run ruff check src/cohezion/ --select RUF012,RUF006` → 0 errors\n  2. After Task 3: `uv run pytest tests/integration/test_surreal_persistence.py -v` → all pass\n  3. After Task 4: Restart cloud-vault-mcp, call `memory_save` then `memory_search` via ToolSearch\n  4. Full suite: `uv run pytest tests/ -q -o \"addopts=\"` → 0 failures\n\n## Risks and Mitigations\n\n| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|-----------|--------|------------|\n| ClassVar annotation breaks Pydantic model serialization | Low | Med | Check if any affected classes are Pydantic models; Pydantic handles ClassVar correctly (excludes from schema) |\n| Storing task references prevents GC of completed tasks | Low | Low | Use `add_done_callback(set.discard)` pattern to auto-remove completed tasks from the set |\n| SurrealDB fixture change makes tests slower (reconnect per test) | Med | Low | Add lightweight sync pre-check to skip module early; each connection is ~50ms |\n| JSONL memory file grows unbounded | Low | Low | Add optional `max_entries` config with FIFO eviction; defer to future if not needed |\n\n## Open Questions\n\n- None — all design decisions resolved via user input.\n\n### Deferred Ideas\n\n- SurrealDB-backed memory store (could replace JSONL for better search performance at scale)\n- Embedding-based semantic search for memory observations (using Ollama embeddings)\n- Memory compaction/summarization for old observations\n"

   122→### Task 2: Fix RUF006 Asyncio Dangling Tasks
   123→
   124→**Objective:** Store references to all 15 fire-and-forget `asyncio.create_task()` calls to prevent tasks from being garbage-collected mid-execution. Each class needs a `_background_tasks: set[asyncio.Task]` attribute and the pattern `task = asyncio.create_task(...); self._background_tasks.add(task); task.add_done_callback(self._background_tasks.discard)`.
   125→
   126→**Dependencies:** None (can run in parallel with Task 1)
   127→
   128→**Files (10 files, all Modify):**
   129→
   130→- `src/cohezion/agents/base.py` (3 violations: lines 129, 560, 819)
   131→- `src/cohezion/cache/semantic_cache.py` (1: line 293)
   132→- `src/cohezion/compound/executor.py` (1: line 840 — uses `asyncio.ensure_future`)
   133→- `src/cohezion/compound/thermal_history_persistence.py` (1: line 93)
   134→- `src/cohezion/compound/thermal_trend_predictor.py` (1: line 113)
   135→- `src/cohezion/core/connection_pool.py` (2: lines 106, 277)
   136→- `src/cohezion/core/multimodal_bridge.py` (1: line 46)
   137→- `src/cohezion/core/task_manager.py` (1: line 166)
   138→- `src/cohezion/cost_optimization/budget_enforcer.py` (1: line 303)
   139→- `src/cohezion/cost_optimization/cost_tracker.py` (1: line 186 — uses `loop.create_task`)
   140→- `src/cohezion/reliability/quantum_performance_monitor.py` (2: lines 263, 467)
   141→- Test: `tests/lint/test_ruf006_dangling_tasks.py` (new)
   142→
   143→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   144→
   145→- Standard pattern for each class:
   146→  ```python
   147→  def __init__(self):
   148→      self._background_tasks: set[asyncio.Task] = set()
   149→
   150→  # Then at each create_task site:
   151→  task = asyncio.create_task(self._some_coroutine())
   152→  self._background_tasks.add(task)
   153→  task.add_done_callback(self._background_tasks.discard)
   154→  ```
   155→- For `executor.py` line 840 which uses `asyncio.ensure_future`: convert to `asyncio.create_task` and apply same pattern
   156→- For `cost_tracker.py` line 186 which uses `loop.create_task`: same pattern but with `loop.create_task`
   157→- For `connection_pool.py` line 106 (in a loop): collect all tasks in a list, then add to set
   158→- Some classes already have `__init__` methods — add the set there. Some don't — add `__init__` or use class-level `_background_tasks: set = set()` (but this would trigger RUF012, so must be in `__init__`)
   159→
   160→**Definition of Done:**
   161→
   162→- [ ] `uv run ruff check src/cohezion/ --select RUF006` reports 0 errors
   163→- [ ] All existing tests still pass
   164→- [ ] New regression test verifies RUF006 count is 0

   169→- `uv run pytest tests/ -q -o "addopts=" -p no:cacheprovider --tb=line 2>&1 | tail -3` — 0 failures

   171→### Task 3: Fix SurrealDB Integration Test Event Loop Issues
   172→
   173→**Objective:** Fix the 6 failures + 23 errors in `tests/integration/test_surreal_persistence.py` caused by `scope="module"` async fixture creating a SurrealDB client on one event loop while individual tests run on different loops.
   174→
   175→**Dependencies:** None (can run in parallel with Tasks 1-2)
   176→
   177→**Files:**
   178→
   179→- Modify: `tests/integration/test_surreal_persistence.py`
   180→
   181→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   182→
   183→- Root cause: `@pytest.fixture(scope="module") async def surreal_client()` creates the client+connection in one event loop. With `pytest-asyncio` (strict mode), each test function gets its own event loop. The SurrealDB client's internal websocket Future is bound to the fixture's loop.
   184→- Fix: Change fixture from `scope="module"` to `scope="function"` (default). Each test gets its own client+connection on its own event loop.
   185→- To avoid reconnecting for every test (slow), add a module-level check that SurrealDB is reachable, then let each test create its own client.
   186→- Add `pytestmark = [pytest.mark.asyncio, pytest.mark.integration]` so tests can be selectively run with `-m integration`
   187→- Keep the `SKIP_INTEGRATION` env var check
   188→- Add a lightweight sync connectivity check at module level that skips the entire module if SurrealDB isn't reachable
   189→
   190→**Definition of Done:**
   191→
   192→- [ ] `uv run pytest tests/integration/test_surreal_persistence.py -v --tb=short` — all tests pass (0 failures, 0 errors)
   193→- [ ] Tests still skip cleanly when SurrealDB is not running
   194→- [ ] Full test suite still passes
   195→
   196→**Verify:**
   197→
   198→- `uv run pytest tests/integration/test_surreal_persistence.py -q --tb=short 2>&1 | tail -5` — 0 failures
   199→- `uv run pytest tests/ -q -o "addopts=" -p no:cacheprovider --tb=line 2>&1 | tail -3` — 0 failures
   200→
   201→### Task 4: Add Persistent Memory Tools to cloud-vault-mcp
   202→
   203→**Objective:** Add 4 new MCP tools (`memory_search`, `memory_save`, `memory_get`, `memory_timeline`) to the cloud-vault-mcp server to replace the lost PILOT `mem-search` functionality. These tools store observations as JSONL entries in `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/memory/observations.jsonl` and support search by text, retrieval by ID, and timeline navigation.
   204→
   205→**Dependencies:** None
   206→
   207→**Files:**
   208→
   209→- Create: `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/memory_store.py` — MemoryStore class with JSONL backend
   210→- Modify: `cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/server.py` — register 4 new tools
   211→- Create: `cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_memory_store.py` — unit tests for MemoryStore
   212→
   213→**Key Decisions / Notes:**
   214→
   215→- **Storage format:** JSONL file at `{vault_path}/memory/observations.jsonl`. Each line is a JSON object:
   216→  ```json
   217→  {"id": 1, "timestamp": "2026-02-23T10:00:00Z", "text": "...", "title": "...", "type": "discovery", "project": "cohezion", "tags": []}
   218→  ```
   219→- **ID assignment:** Auto-incrementing integer (max ID from file + 1). Thread-safe via file lock.
   220→- **Search:** Full-text substring match on `text` + `title` fields, with optional filters: `type`, `project`, `dateStart`, `dateEnd`, `limit`. Returns index entries (id, title, timestamp, snippet) for token efficiency.
   221→- **Get:** Fetch full observation details by list of IDs.
   222→- **Timeline:** Given an anchor ID or query, return chronological observations around it (configurable `depth_before`, `depth_after`).
   223→- **Tool signatures** (matching old mem-search API from `~/.claude/rules/memory.md`):
   224→  - `memory_search(query: str, limit: int = 20, type: str = "", project: str = "", dateStart: str = "", dateEnd: str = "") → str`
   225→  - `memory_save(text: str, title: str = "", project: str = "cohezion", type: str = "discovery") → str`
   226→  - `memory_get(ids: list[int]) → str`
   227→  - `memory_timeline(anchor: int = 0, query: str = "", depth_before: int = 5, depth_after: int = 5) → str`
   228→- Follow existing server.py pattern: import module, create instance, register tools with `@mcp.tool()`
   229→- JSONL chosen over SurrealDB to avoid adding another DB dependency and to ensure the memory file is portable/inspectable
   230→
   231→**Definition of Done:**
   232→
   233→- [ ] 4 new MCP tools registered in cloud-vault-mcp server
   234→- [ ] MemoryStore handles save, search, get, timeline operations
   235→- [ ] JSONL file created at `{vault_path}/memory/observations.jsonl`
   236→- [ ] Unit tests cover all 4 operations + edge cases (empty store, missing IDs, date filtering)
   237→- [ ] Tools return JSON matching the 3-layer workflow from `memory.md`
   238→
   239→**Verify:**
   240→
   241→- `uv run pytest cloud-vault-mcp/tests/test_memory_store.py -q` — all tests pass
   242→- `uv run ruff check cloud-vault-mcp/src/mcp_server/memory_store.py` — 0 lint errors

   285→## Risks and Mitigations
   286→
   287→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
   288→|------|-----------|--------|------------|
   289→| ClassVar annotation breaks Pydantic model serialization | Low | Med | Check if any affected classes are Pydantic models; Pydantic handles ClassVar correctly (excludes from schema) |
   290→| Storing task references prevents GC of completed tasks | Low | Low | Use `add_done_callback(set.discard)` pattern to auto-remove completed tasks from the set |
   291→| SurrealDB fixture change makes tests slower (reconnect per test) | Med | Low | Add lightweight sync pre-check to skip module early; each connection is ~50ms |
   292→| JSONL memory file grows unbounded | Low | Low | Add optional `max_entries` config with FIFO eviction; defer to future if not needed |

With `pytest-asyncio`, each test function gets its own event loop. The SurrealDB client's internal websocket Future is bound to the fixture's loop, causing \"Future attached to a different loop\" errors.\n- Root cause (part 2): The project's `pyproject.toml` does not set `asyncio_mode`, which defaults to `strict` mode. In strict mode, every async test function must be explicitly marked with `@pytest.mark.asyncio`. Without it, async tests fail to run.\n- Fix 1: Change fixture from `scope=\"module\"` to `scope=\"function\"` (default). Each test gets its own client+connection on its own event loop.\n- Fix 2: Add `pytestmark = [pytest.mark.asyncio, pytest.mark.integration]` at module level — this provides the required asyncio mark for all async tests AND allows running with `-m integration`. **Both fixes are required; neither alone is sufficient.**\n- To avoid reconnecting for every test (slow), add a lightweight sync pre-check at module level (using `socket.connect`) that skips the entire module early if SurrealDB isn't reachable.\n- Keep the `SKIP_INTEGRATION` env var check alongside the connectivity check"

{"findings": [{"severity": "must_fix", "issue": "Task 5 timing is architecturally broken: the worktree constraint note says to edit ~/.claude/rules/ files 'after Task 4 changes are synced to the base branch via worktree sync', but worktree sync is the FINAL step in spec-verify — not something that happens mid-plan. Task 5 depends on Task 4, but the rule files live outside the repo. If the implementer edits them inside the worktree sync phase, changes will be lost or the sync will leave the repo inconsistent. The plan provides no mechanism for editing out-of-repo files during spec-verify's squash merge.", "recommendation": "Explicitly note that Task 5 edits happen POST-WORKTREE-SYNC, as a manual follow-up step after the plan reaches VERIFIED status. Mark Task 5 with a clear 'Post-merge manual step' tag so the implementer does not attempt it inside the worktree."}, {"severity": "must_fix", "issue": "RUF006 fix for base.py (3 violations at lines 129, 560, 819) assumes a single __init__ exists and can receive the _background_tasks set, but BaseAgent is likely subclassed by many agents. Adding self._background_tasks in BaseAgent.__init__ and using it at those 3 call sites is safe only if subclasses call super().__init__(). The plan does not verify super().__init__() call presence in generated agents (src/cohezion/agents/generated/skill_0_agent.py, skill_1_agent.py) or factory-created agents.", "recommendation": "Verify all BaseAgent subclasses call super().__init__(). If any do not, add a defensive fallback: getattr(self, '_background_tasks', set()) before adding to the set, or initialize in __init_subclass__."}, {"severity": "should_fix", "issue": "Task 3 adds a lightweight socket pre-check to skip the module early if SurrealDB is unreachable, but the plan describes the root cause as a scope='module' fixture creating a connection bound to a different event loop. Changing to scope='function' means each test reconnects. The plan states ~50ms per connection but does not account for teardown. With 6 tests x 50ms connect + teardown overhead, suite time increases ~600ms minimum. More critically, if the socket pre-check passes (SurrealDB port open) but authentication fails (wrong credentials), tests will error instead of skip — the pre-check only validates TCP reachability, not auth.", "recommendation": "Add a lightweight async auth probe in the skip check (attempt a single authenticated query in a try/except) rather than just socket connectivity."}, {"severity": "should_fix", "issue": "The memory_timeline tool signature uses anchor: int = 0 with 0 as sentinel for 'no anchor'. ID 0 is also a valid auto-increment ID if observations.jsonl starts at ID 0. This creates an ambiguity: anchor=0 means 'no anchor, use query' but could also mean 'anchor to observation #0'.", "recommendation": "Use anchor: int | None = None as the sentinel, and require either anchor or query to be provided. Raise ValueError if both are None."}, {"severity": "should_fix", "issue": "JSONL file concurrency is handled via filelock for thread safety, but the plan targets async code throughout. asyncio + filelock (which is synchronous blocking I/O) will block the event loop during disk writes. The plan does not address this mismatch — MemoryStore methods will likely be called from async MCP tool handlers.", "recommendation": "Wrap filelock I/O in asyncio.to_thread() or use aiofiles with an asyncio Lock. Document the concurrency model explicitly in memory_store.py."}, {"severity": "suggestion", "issue": "The regression tests for RUF012 and RUF006 (test_ruf006_dangling_tasks.py, test_ruf012_classvar.py) will run ruff as a subprocess to assert 0 violations. This creates a slow CI dependency on the ruff binary being in PATH inside uv run pytest. If ruff is not installed as a dev dependency or PATH differs in CI, these tests silently pass despite violations.", "recommendation": "Add ruff to dev dependencies in pyproject.toml and assert its presence in the test module, or use subprocess with explicit failure on FileNotFoundError rather than silently skipping."}, {"severity": "suggestion", "issue": "Task 4 JSONL search is full-text substring match only. The memory.md workflow documentation refers to 'type' filter values like 'bugfix', 'feature', 'refactor', 'discovery', 'decision', 'change'. The plan does not enforce that memory_save validates the 'type' field against this enum. Invalid type values saved now will silently corrupt future type-filtered searches.", "recommendation": "Add type validation in MemoryStore.save() against the defined enum values, with a ValueError on invalid type."}], "summary": "Plan is generally well-structured with correct technical analysis of all four debt categories. Two must-fix issues: Task 5 has a broken execution model (out-of-repo files cannot be edited inside a worktree sync cycle) and the RUF006 BaseAgent fix has an untested super().__init__() assumption across generated subclasses. Three should-fix issues around auth vs TCP probe for SurrealDB skip, anchor=0 sentinel ambiguity in timeline, and asyncio/filelock event loop blocking. The plan correctly avoids ruff --unsafe-fixes and correctly places asyncio primitives in __init__. Core architecture is sound."}

  "new_string": "- **Tool signatures** (matching old mem-search API from `~/.claude/rules/memory.md`):\n  - `memory_search(query: str, limit: int = 20, type: str = \"\", project: str = \"\", dateStart: str = \"\", dateEnd: str = \"\") → str`\n  - `memory_save(text: str, title: str = \"\", project: str = \"cohezion\", type: str = \"discovery\") → str` — validates `type` against allowed enum (`bugfix`, `feature`, `refactor`, `discovery`, `decision`, `change`); raises `ValueError` on invalid value\n  - `memory_get(ids: list[int]) → str`\n  - `memory_timeline(anchor: int | None = None, query: str = \"\", depth_before: int = 5, depth_after: int = 5) → str` — requires either `anchor` or `query`; raises `ValueError` if both are None\n- Follow existing server.py pattern: import module, create instance, register tools with `@mcp.tool()`\n- JSONL chosen over SurrealDB to avoid adding another DB dependency and to ensure the memory file is portable/inspectable\n- **Thread/async safety:** MemoryStore I/O uses `filelock.FileLock` for write serialization. Since MCP tools are called from an async context, all blocking I/O in MemoryStore must be wrapped with `asyncio.to_thread()` inside the tool handlers in `server.py`. MemoryStore itself stays synchronous (simpler, fully testable); the async wrapper lives in server.py."
}

  "new_string": "- Standard pattern for async methods (most sites):\n  ```python\n  def __init__(self):\n      self._background_tasks: set[asyncio.Task] = set()\n\n  # Then at each create_task site inside async methods:\n  task = asyncio.create_task(self._some_coroutine())\n  self._background_tasks.add(task)\n  task.add_done_callback(self._background_tasks.discard)\n  ```\n- **Special case — `base.py:129` and `connection_pool.py:106` (create_task called from `__init__`):** These sites call `asyncio.create_task()` directly from synchronous `__init__` code, which raises `RuntimeError: no current event loop` in sync contexts. Fix: wrap with `try: loop = asyncio.get_running_loop(); task = loop.create_task(...)` so it only fires when a loop is already running. Move actual startup logic to a separate `async def start()` if needed. The task-reference storage fix alone does NOT fix the structural sync-context problem.\n- **Special case — `connection_pool.py:_initialize_pool`:** This sync method calls `asyncio.create_task()` in a loop from `__init__`. Convert `_initialize_pool` to `async def _initialize_pool()` and call it lazily on first `acquire()` call instead of from `__init__`. This is a structural async fix that must precede the task-reference storage fix.\n- For `executor.py` line 840 which uses `asyncio.ensure_future`: convert to `asyncio.create_task` and apply same pattern\n- For `cost_tracker.py` line 186 which uses `loop.create_task`: convert to `asyncio.create_task` (deprecated in Python 3.10+, project targets 3.13+) and apply same pattern\n- For `connection_pool.py` line 277: apply standard pattern once line 106 structural fix is in place\n- Some classes already have `__init__` methods — add the set there. Some don't — add `__init__` or use class-level `_background_tasks: set = set()` (but this would trigger RUF012, so must be in `__init__`)\n- **BaseAgent subclass check:** `src/cohezion/agents/base.py` violations (lines 129, 560, 819) add `self._background_tasks` in `BaseAgent.__init__`. Before implementing, verify that all known subclasses (`src/cohezion/agents/generated/skill_0_agent.py`, `skill_1_agent.py`, and factory-created agents) call `super().__init__()`. Add a defensive `self._background_tasks = getattr(self, '_background_tasks', set())` fallback if any subclass omits `super().__init__()`."
}

Add a clear docstring to each new tool explaining the distinction: memory tools store cross-session observations; `push_memory` syncs MEMORY.md to vault sections. Do not deprecate `push_memory`.\n- **max_entries default:** Implement FIFO eviction at `max_entries=10_000` (default). Document O(N) search behaviour at that ceiling in the module docstring."

```
## Summary

I've diagnosed the bug in `cz context --json`. Here's the issue:

### **The Bug**
File: `/home/mike-anderson/vaults/cohezion-vault/tools/cohezion-engine/src/cohezion_engine/context.py` (lines 77-79)

The code **incorrectly sums three incompatible token metrics**:
```python
total_tokens += usage.get("input_tokens", 0)                    # ✓ Correct
total_tokens += usage.get("cache_creation_input_tokens", 0)     # ✗ Wrong
total_tokens += usage.get("cache_read_input_tokens", 0)         # ✗ Wrong
```

### **Why It's Wrong**

1. **`input_tokens` (48,641)** — Actual tokens consumed from the context window
   - Should be counted ✓

2. **`cache_creation_input_tokens` (2.5M)** — Tokens written to cache storage
   - Billed once, then reused for free
   - Don't consume context going forward
   - Should NOT be counted ✗

3. **`cache_read_input_tokens` (103.7M)** — Cumulative cache hits across the session
   - These are reuses of previously cached tokens
   - Don't consume new context at all
   - Adding this inflates the total by **500x**
   - Should NOT be counted ✗

### **The Math**

**Current buggy calculation:**
- Sum: 48,641 + 2,569,783 + 103,715,708 = 106,334,132 tokens
- Percentage: (106,334,132 / 200,000) × 100 = **53,167%**
- Status: False "CLEAR_NEEDED" warning

**Correct calculation:**
- Sum: 48,641 tokens (input_tokens only)
- Percentage: (48,641 / 200,000) × 100 = **24.32%**
- Status: Should be "OK"

### **Impact**
- Context monitoring is off by ~500x
- Users get false session continuation warnings
- Makes the tool unreliable for actual context awareness

The diagnosis is saved in `REDACTED.md` with complete evidence and calculations.
agentId: adf0868 (for resuming to continue this agent's work if needed)
<usage>total_tokens: 67473
tool_uses: 13
duration_ms: 78292</usage>
```

> AGENT

Found the bug. Here's the diagnosis:

**`cz context` calculates 45,951% because it sums three incompatible token fields:**

```python
# context.py — current (buggy)
total_tokens += usage.get("input_tokens", 0)               # 48K — correct
total_tokens += usage.get("cache_creation_input_tokens", 0) # 2.5M — WRONG (write cost, not context)  
total_tokens += usage.get("cache_read_input_tokens", 0)    # 103M — WRONG (cumulative cache hits)
```

`cache_read_input_tokens` inflates the total ~500x. Only `input_tokens` counts toward the context window. Real context usage is ~24%, not 45,951%.

The fix is a 2-line change in `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/tools/cohezion-engine/src/cohezion_engine/context.py`.

    "E",     # pycodestyle errors
    "F",     # pyflakes

    "I",     # isort

    "UP",    # pyupgrade
    "S",     # flake8-bandit (security)
    "B",     # flake8-bugbear
    "A",     # flake8-builtins
    "C4",    # flake8-comprehensions
    "SIM",   # flake8-simplify
    "TCH",   # flake8-type-checking
    "RUF",   # Ruff-specific rules
]

ignore = [
    # Security false positives: simulation/testing code uses non-cryptographic random
    "S311",   # suspicious-non-cryptographic-random-usage (random for simulation, not security)
    # Subprocess rules: infrastructure scripts legitimately use partial paths and subprocess
    "S603",   # subprocess-without-shell-equals-true
    "S607",   # start-process-with-partial-path
    # Unicode symbols in comments are intentional (Greek/math notation)
    "RUF003", # ambiguous-unicode-character-comment
    # SurrealDB queries are parameterized - S608 false positive (not traditional SQL injection risk)
    "S608",   # hardcoded-sql-expression
    # Uppercase variables in physics/ML code are conventional (X, Y, Z, T, R, etc.)
    "N806",   # non-lowercase-variable-in-function
    # Hardcoded temp files in simulation/testing helpers - not production security risk
    "S108",   # hardcoded-temp-file

    "TC001",  # typing-only-first-party-import
    "TC002",  # typing-only-third-party-import
    "TC003",  # typing-only-standard-library-import
]

# Per-file customization
[tool.ruff.lint.per-file-ignores]
"tests/**/*.py" = [
    "S101",  # Allow assert in tests
